# Gherkin mapping

| Classification | Scenario declarations |
| --- | --- |
| Below the UI | 49 |
| Conflicting | 1 |
| Mapped | 1470 |

No scenario was called stale solely because a UI signal was missing. The “Conflicting” example below exposes an action hidden inside an outcome check; every expanded row still has a proposed chart journey. Mapped means the agreed linear example has a replayable chart path, not that the application was executed or all branches were confirmed.

## Transitions without a directly mapped Gherkin journey

A model trace is not a Gherkin example. The rows below list every transition not taken by a journey with a `gherkin` reference in that same chart. Some are mirrors of agreed examples in sibling charts; those need a binding, while novel failures and interruption reactions need product examples. “No direct journey” does not mean no feature anywhere mentions a related behavior.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
